and

that

01:30:41 --> 01:30:46
here's a huge amount of integrated

of

is this sort of thought that does we

causes

totally random and senseless like the

so i think there was a good answer to

to that was

what makes the difference between a free

action

02:26:05 --> 02:26:10
random event

02:26:06 --> 02:26:12
is that free choice involves

02:26:09 --> 02:26:14
responsiveness to reasons

um so again we've talked about this

earlier

02:26:15 --> 02:26:20
if i'm deciding whether to take a job in

ard of life maybe or being close to

i you know edge towards

02:26:30 --> 02:26:35
a decision so

02:26:32 --> 02:26:37
i think that is sufficient to

02:26:34 --> 02:26:38
distinguish it

02:26:36 --> 02:26:40
um you know

02:26:38 --> 02:26:42
e're hypothetically supposing

02:26:40 --> 02:26:43
trying to make sense of this idea not

nough to distinguish it from a

decision involved

02:26:51 --> 02:26:56
